Anadyomene stellata · 498751

PASS

Green seaweed with no stated native origin, recorded across the Mediterranean and tropical and subtropical Atlantic and Indo-Pacific. Its pale green to yellowish, semi-transparent blades are wavy, firm and brittle, with a memorable fan-like pattern of veins. In the Mediterranean it generally grows on hard surfaces or seagrass rhizomes near the surface, though Caribbean records reportedly reach about 90 metres deep.
